The most effective way in combating drought and extremism is to plant and increase our forest existence and run our water resources optimum. The rains in our country differ from the seasons. Waters in the rainy season have to be used in dry season. Therefore, we have to build dams on rivers and collect water and use it in dry season. Our dams also provide energy production, agricultural irrigation, drinking and use water, as well as reducing our agricultural areas and preventing major economic losses. Some people are in a bad mood against the dams. The dams are projects that will pay back investment from five to ten years, which will benefit the economy from at least a hundred years to 200 years. For example, the Karakaya Dam was activated in 1987, producing 131 billion dollars in energy. The Ataturk Dam has been established in 1993, producing 110 billion in energy so far, and is storing water to water 922,000 hectares of land. The Deeper Dam was put into business in 2013, produced 6 billion dollars of energy and paid back the investment, and will continue to print money. The Ilisu Dam is finished and the water has begun to catch, producing 2.5 billion dollars of energy a year. On one hand, irrigation projects are wanted, and on the other hand, dams are opposed. tbmm-2019-12-16sit06spe0576par0324	Thank you, Mr. President. I answer the question of Mr. Ali Cumhur Taşkın. ▪ Konya-Karaman says we want the fast train, Adana-Mersin, to be completed. We are planning the completion of the 102km Conium-Karaman Speed Train Project in the second quarter of 2020 and moving to speed train management. We aim to complete the 135km Karaman-Turkish section in 2022, and the Yulky-News section in 2025. Again, preparations for construction of the 67km road between Adana and Mersin continue, aimed at completion of the project in 2022. Ali Öztunç's question is: Planes cannot land at the Kahramanmarash Airport, flights are cancelled every day of the week. There are reciprocal flights to Istanbul Airport and Sabiha Gökçen Airport for five days a week and Ankara Esenboğa Airport for four days a week. International standards include VOR, DME and NDB navigational devices. Why isn't Mr. Honorary Adjective? Why isn't there sufficient investment in Fiber? I answer: Our country has a 20 percent annual growth rate in the broadband service starting in 2010, and 3.1 million subscribers have been reached as of today. In line with this development in the number of subscribers, infrastructure has also evolved in the same way. The number of households reached at 9 million in the first half of 2015 has now exceeded 14 million and has increased by 55 per cent in the last four years. Our fiber infrastructure length in this period has also increased by 40 per cent and has exceeded 371,000km. The Electronic Telecommunications Infrastructure Information System has been established and provided a single database of electronic communications infrastructure for the operators. Thanks to EHAB, businesses are expected to increase our fiber grid length and subscriber numbers much faster with the ease and acceleration of infrastructure installations. When will the road of Mr. Olcay's guide be completed in Mersin-Antalia and Silifke-Mut? I answer: The Mediterranean Coast Road Project, which continues to be built between Mersin-Antalia, is 479km long and will be reduced to 439km by the completion of the road. 399 kilometers of the road is complete. We aim to end the entire project in 2023. The Karaman-Mut-Silifke road was a total of 100 miles [50 km] and was completed as a divided road in the previous years. Road construction continues in five sections, with a total length of 51km between Karaman and Silifke. A total of eight tunnels, along with Sertavul Tunnel, are under way, aimed at the completion of the route in 2022. The question of Mr. Dursun Atash is: how is the high-speed train project to Kayseri? (The project work of the Yerköy-Kayseri High Speed Train Project is completed and the tender work is under way. In the coming days, we'll make a tender and start working on it. Again, the question of Dursun Atash is: there have been many accidents on the way to Kayseri-Malatia. He says, "When will the path of the fountain-Round be taken into the investment programme?" The road of the Fountain-Round is divided into traffic. Project work covering the Mazardırın Tunnel is under way between the Fountain and is then proposed to the investment programme and planned for budget opportunities. Thank you.
